BY CHIEF OFFICER
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCO-2025/S.NO.242/UD-14/2025.----- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9,
10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking
into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Extra Ordinary No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Shirdi Municipal Council. The number
and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided, as shown in the
Schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government Maharashtra, or its authorised officer, with
the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authourised officer, hereby makes
the following Notification.

Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, as specified for Shirdi Municipal
Council, under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards as
follows.--
Total number of councillors:- 23
Two councillors wards :- 10
Three councillors wards :- 01
Total wards :- 11
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government
Maharashtra, or it’sauthorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, hereby hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into
which the said Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to
this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

SANGAMNER MUNICIPAL COUNCIL
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCO-2025/Pra.No.242/UD-14/2025.---- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9,
10 and 341-B ofthe Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. 14, dated
18th August 2025 fixing for the Sangamner Municipal Council, the number and the extent of the wards
into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the Schedule to said Draft Notification.
The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notificaion.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Sangamner Municipal Council
under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
Total number of councillors - 30
Two councillors wards -15
Three councillors wards - 00
Total wards -15
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCO-2025/Pra.Kra.-242/UD-14/2025.---- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
9, 10 and 341-B ofthe Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One - A, Nashik Division Supplement, dated
18th August 2025 fixing for the Rahata Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into
which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The
State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Rahata Municipal Council under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
Total number of councillors - 20
Two councillors wards -10
Three councillors wards - 00
Total wards -10
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A dated August 18th, 2025 fixing for the

municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.

Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Rahuri Municipal Council, Nagar

of wards is as follows :-

Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised

This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCOPra.Kra.-UD-/2025. In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
, and -B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, (Mah. Act No. XL of hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
MAHBIL/13/2/2011-TC, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Shrirampur Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Shrirampur Municipal Council
under Sections and and 341-B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
Total number of councillors - 34
Two councillors wards - 17
Three councillors wards - 00
Total wards. - 17
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Councils
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. ../-/ 2025.-- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9, 10 / -
and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. 14, dated
18th August 2025 fixing for the Shevgaon Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards
into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification.
The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Shevgaon Municipal Council,
under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 24
2. Two councillors wards - 12
3. Three councillors wards - 00
4. Total wards - 12
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council,
Nagar Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held
immediately following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No.MCO/P.C No. 2025/242/NAVI-14/2025 In exercise of the powers conferred by the
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act. No. XL of 1965) (hereafter referred to as the “said Act”) after carefully
taking into consideration the objections and suggestion received in response to the Draft Notification
Extraordinary No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Amalner Municipal Council. The number
and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
Schedule to this Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorized officer with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorized officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission , Maharashtra or it’s authorized officer, as specified for Amalner Municipal Council under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the number of councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors : 36
2. Two councillors wards : 18
3. Three councillors wards : 00
4. Total wards : 18
Now, therefore in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorized officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council,
Nagar Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of the Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCO-2025/Pra.Kra.242/UD-14/2025.--- The State Government with approval of State
Election Commission, Maharashtra proposed to make the following Draft Notification under Sections 9,
10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Annexure-8, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Bhadgaon Municipal Council. The number and the
extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said
Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Bhadgaon Municipal Council,
under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 24
2. Two councillors wards - 12
3. Three councillors wards - 00
4. Total wards - 12
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DCNSK-31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMA E-2483421/2025.--- In exercise of the powers
conferred by Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act")
after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft
Notification No., Extraordinary No. 14, Maharashtra Government Gazette Extraordinary, Part One-A,
Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Nashirabad Municipal Council.
The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in
the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorized officer, with the
Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the
following Notification.

Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or It’s authorized officer, as specified for Nashirabad Municipal Council
under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 20
2. Two councillors wards - 10
3. Total wards - 10
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

Yawal Municipal Council, Yawal
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DCNSK/31015(11)/39/DRDMA-E-2483421/2025.--- In exercise of the powers conferred by
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully
taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification,
Goverment of Maharashtra Extra Ordinary Part one- A, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Yawal
Municipal Council the number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be
divided, as shown in the Schedule to this Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised
officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commision, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer,
hereby, makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorized officer, as specified for Yawal Municipal Council under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 23
2. Two councillors wards - 10
3. Three councillors wards - 01
4. Total wards - 11
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

BY CHIEF OFFICER
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. CME/Election/101/2025.----- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9, 10 and
341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
(Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. Extra
Ordinary No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Chalisgaon Municipal Council. The number
and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided, as shown in the
Schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government Maharashtra, or it’s authorised officer, with
the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authourised officer, hereby makes
the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authourised officer, as specified for Chalisgaon Municipal
Council, under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards as
follows.--
Total number of councillors:- 36
Two councillors wards :- 18
Three councillors wards :- 00
Total wards :- 18
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government
Maharashtra, or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, hereby hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into
which the said Municipal Council area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DCNSK/31015(11)19/2025-DRDMAE-2483421/2025.--- In exercise of the powers
conferred by Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act")
after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft
Notification No. Extraordinary No. 14, Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A
Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Varangaon Municipal Council.
The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in
the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the
Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the
following Notification.

Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Varangaon Municipal Council,
under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 21

Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.

following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCO-2025/M.No.242/UD-14/2025.--- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9, 10
and 341-B of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. RNI No.
MAHBIL/13/2/2011-TC, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Erandol Municipal Council. The number
and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorized officer, as specified for Erandol Municipal Council under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 23
2. Two councillors wards - 10
3. Three councillors wards - 01
4. Total wards - 11
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DGNSKS-31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMA E-2483421/2025.---- In exercise of the powers
conferred by Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act")
after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft
Notification No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Pachora Municipal Council, Pachora. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.

Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Pachora Municipal Council,
Pachora under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 28
2. Two councillors wards - 14
3. Three councillors wards - 00
4. Total wards - 14
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council,
Nagar Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965
No. - DCNSK-31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMA E-2483421/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred
by Sections 9, 10 & 341-B of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Township Act, (Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking
into consideration the objection & suggestion received in response to the Draft Notification No. 14,
dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Jamner Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the
wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Jamner Municipal Council
under Sections 9, 10 & 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors :- 26
2. Two councillors wards :- 13
3. Total wards :- 13
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 & 341-B of The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. SMC/GAD/Gen.Election-2025/733/2025.-- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. Extra
Ordinary No.14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Savda Municipal Council. The number and the
extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said
Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Savda Municipal Council, under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-

Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.

following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965
No. - MCO-2025/pra.kra.242/UD-14/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred by sections 9, 10
& 341-B of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act,
(Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. 14, dated
18th August 2025 fixing for the Parola Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into
which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The
State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Parola Municipal Council under
sections 9, 10 & 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councilors :- 24
2. Two councillors wards :- 12
3. Three councillors wards :- 00
4. Total wards :- 12

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under sections 9, 10 & 341-B of The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. RMC/GAD/Gen. Election-2025/1324/2025.--- In exercise of the powers conferred by
Sections , and -B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, (Mah. Act No. XL of hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully
taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification
No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Raver Municipal Council. The number and the extent of
the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the Schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Raver Municipal Council under
Sections and and 341-B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
Total number of councillors - 24
Two councillors wards - 12
Three councillors wards - 00
Total wards. - 12
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the Schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCOPra.Kra.-UD-/2025. In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
, and -B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, (Mah. Act No. XL of hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
MAHBIL/13/2/2011-TC, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Dharangaon Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Dharangaon Municipal Council
under Sections and and 341-B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
Total number of councillors - 23
Two councillors wards - 10
Three councillors wards - 01
Total wards. - 11
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. BMC/Election-2025/58/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9, 10 and
341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
(Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Final Notification No. MCO-
2025/Pra.Kra.242/UD-14, dated 10th June 2025 fixing for the Bhusawal Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Final Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval
of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, as specified for Bhusawal Municipal
Council, under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards as
follows.--
Total number of councillors:- 50
Two councillors wards :- 25
Three councillors wards :- 00
Total wards :- 25

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government
Maharashtra or it’sauthorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, hereby hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into
which the said Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to
this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. M.C.O.-2025/CN-242/UD-14/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9, 10
and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act”) after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. M.C.O.-
2025/CN-242/UD-14/2025, Dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Chopda Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
scheduled to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorized officer, with the Approval
of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorized officer hereby makes the following
Notification.

Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, as specified for Chopada Municipal
Council, under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards as
follows.--
Total number of councillors:- 31
Two councillors wards :- 14
Three councillors wards :- 01
Total wards :- 15
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government
Maharashtra or it’sauthorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the
said Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this
Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

Sanjay Jadhav, to the east south corner of the old
Dhanwadi Road.
South:- Starting from the south-east corner of the
canal, heading westward along the canal up to the
south-east corner of the Municipal Council limit, from
there southward, bypassing Gut No. 123, then
proceeding westward along the new Dharangaon road,
passing through the south-east corner of Gut No. 1258,
then westward, bypassing Gut No. 1262, then heading
northward up to the south-west corner of Gut No. 1289,
from there westward up to the south-west corner of Gut
No. 1294, following the Municipal Council boundary up
to Chahardi Road, then northward from the south-west
corner of Gut No. 1429, and finally reaching the south-
west corner of Gut No. 1439.
West:- Starting from the south-west corner of Gut No.
1439 along the Municipal Council boundary, heading
northward up to the south-west corner of Gut No. 1445,
then roceeding westward, bypassing Gut No. 1444, and
heading northward up to State Highway No. 4, which
passes through Gut No. 1510.
In case of any discrepancy in description of ward in the Marathi and English Notification, the
description as per boundaries shown in the map shall be treated as valid.
A map of the Chopda Municipal Council showing the limits of the wards is available during
office hours for inspection in the office of the Chopda Municipal Council.

RAMNIVAS ZANWAR,
Chief Officer,
Chopda Municipal Council,
Chopda, District Jalgaon.
Chopda, 26th September 2025.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. NMP/GAD/Gen.Election-2025/763/2025.--- In exercise of the powers conferred by
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully
taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification
No. Extra Ordinary No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Muktainagar Nagar Panchayat. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Muktainagar Nagar Panchayat,
under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 17
2. Two councillors wards - 00
3. Three councillors wards - 00
4. Total wards - 17
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Nagar Panchayat
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965

The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in
the Schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the
Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the
following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Shendurni Nagar Panchayat
under Sections and and 341-B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
Total number of councillors - 17
2Total wards. - 17
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Nagar Panchayat
area shall be divided as shown in the Schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965

1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Maharashtra Government Gazette, -- Extraordinary Part One-A, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the
Bhagur Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area
shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its
authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised
officer, hereby makes the following Notification.

Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Bhagur Municipal Council, under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-

Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council area shall
be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

office hours for inspection in the office of the Bhagur Municipal Council.

Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965

extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said
Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election

Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. ONP/NIVADNUK/KARYA-9492025. In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections ,
1 and -B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking
into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
MCO -Pra.Kra. -UD -14/Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One -
A, Nashik Division Supplement, dated 18th August2025 fixing for theOzar Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval
of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Ozar Municipal Council under
Sections , and 341 -B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows - -
Total number of councillors - 27
Two councillors wards - 12
Three councillors wards - 01
Total wards. - 12
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State
Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or it’s authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal
Council area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965
No. - MCO-2025/pra.kr.242/UD-14/2025, In exercise of the powers conferred by sections 9, 10
& 341-B of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act,
(Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Government Of Maharashtra, Gazette, Extra-Ordinary Part-A, Nashik Regional Suppliment, No-14
dated-18 August 2025 fixing for the Pimpalgaon Baswant Municipal Council. The number and the
extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said
Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
2. Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Pimpalgaon Baswant
Municipal Council under sections 9, 10 & 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar
Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of
wards is as follows :-
Total number of councilors = 25
Two councillors wards = 11
Three councillors wards = 01
Total wards = 12
3. Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under sections 9, 10 & 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal
Council area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.

4. This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held
immediately following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965
No. - MCO-2025/pra.kr.242/UD-14/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred by sections 9, 10
& 341-B of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act,
(Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Government Of Maharashtra, Gazette, Extra-Ordinary Part-A, Nashik Regional Suppliment, No-14
dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Chandwad Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the
wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.

2. Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Chandwad Municipal
Council under sections 9, 10 & 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councilors :- 20
2. Two councillors wards :- 10
3. Three councillors wards :- 00
4. Total wards :- 10
3. Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under sections 9, 10 & 341-B of The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
4. This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held
immediately following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. - 682/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 9, 10 & 341-B of The
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, (Mah. Act No.XL of
1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the objection &
suggestion received in response to the Draft Notification No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the
Jamner Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal
area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its
authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer, hereby makes the following Notification.

Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Jamner Municipal Council
under Sections 9, 10 & 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors :- 26
2. Two councillors wards :- 13
3. Three councillors wards :- 00
4. Total wards :- 13
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 & 341-B of The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCOPra.Kra.-UD-/2025.--- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
, 1 and -B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking
into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One - A, Nashik Division Supplement, dated
18/08/2025 fixing for the Manmad Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into
which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The
State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Manmad Municipal Council under
Sections , and 341-B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
Total number of councillors - 33
Two councillors wards - 15
Three councillors wards - 01
Total wards. - 16
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State
Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or it’s authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Manmad
Municipal Councils area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCOPra.Kra.-UD-/2025. In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
, and -B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, (Mah. Act No. XL of hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking
into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.
Government of Maharashtra, Gazzete, Extra-Ordinary Part-A, Nashik Regional Suppliment, No. 14
dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Satana Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the
wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Satana Municipal Council under
Sections and and 341-B of theMaharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
Total number of councillors - 24
Two councillors wards - 12
Three councillors wards - 00
Total wards. - 12
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections , and -B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. MCO-2025/Pra. Kra. 242/UD-14/2025.----- In exercise of the powers conferred by Sections
9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking
into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification,
Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part 1-A, Nashik Divisional supplement, Shravan 27,
Shake 1947, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Dondaicha Varwade Municipal Council,
Dondaicha. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be
divided, as shown in the Schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government Maharashtra or
it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authourised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, as specified for Dondaicha Varwade
Municipal Council, under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar
Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number
of wards as follows.--
Total number of councillors:- 26
Two councillors wards :- 13
Three councillors wards :- --
Total wards :- 13
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government
Maharashtra, or it’sauthorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, hereby hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into
which the said Municipal Council area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DCNSK/31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMAE2483421/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred
by Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully
taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification
No. DCNSK/31015(11)/19/2025-DRDMAE2287579, Dated 14th August 2025 fixing for the Pimpalner
Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be
divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorized
officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorized officer,
hereby makes the following notification ;
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, as specified for Pimpalner Municipal
Council under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards as
follows.-
Total number of councillors:- 20
Two councillors wards :- 10
Three councillors wards :- --
Total wards :- 10

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government
Maharashtra or it’sauthorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authourised officer, hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the
said Municipal Council area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DCNSK-31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMA E-2483421/2025.-In exercise of the powers
conferred by Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act No. XL1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act”)
after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the
Draft Notification No. DCNSK/31015(11)/19/2025-DRDMA E 2287579/2025, dated 18th August 2025
fixing for the Nandurbar Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the
said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Nandurbar Municipal
Council, under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is
as follows :-
Total number of councillors - 41
Two councillors wards - 19
Three Councillors wards - 01
Total wards - 20
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal
Council, area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held
immediately following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. DCNSK-31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMA-E-2483421/2025.--- In exercise of the powers
conferred by the Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965 (Mah. Act. No. XL of 1965) (hereafter referred to as the “said Act”)
after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft
Notification No. DCNSK/31015 (11)/19/DRDMA-E-2287579/2025, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for
the Taloda Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal
area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s
authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised
officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorized officer, as specified for Taloda Municipal Council under
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the number of councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors : 21
2. Two councillors wards : 09
3. Three councillors wards : 01
4. Total wards : 10
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965, the State Government or
it’s authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorized officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of the Notification.

hours for inspection in the office of the Taloda Municipal Council.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. - DCNSK-31015(11)/39/2025-DRDMA E-2483421/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred
by Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of he Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act,1965 (Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after
carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received in response to the Draft
Notification No. Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extra-Ordinary Part-A, Nashik Divisional
Supplement, page No. 448 to 466, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Shahada Municipal Council.
The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown
in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the
Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the
following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Shahada Municipal
Council under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats
and Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-
1. Total number of councillors :- 29
2. Two councillors wards :- 13
3. Three councillors wards :- 01
4. Total wards :- 14

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the Shahada Municipal
Council area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
No. - DCNSK-31015(11)/39/DRDMA-E-2483421/2025.- In exercise of the powers conferred by
Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial
Townships Act,1965 (Mah. Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after
carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received in response to the Draft
Notification No. Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extra-Ordinary Part-A, Nashik Divisional
Supplement No. 14, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Navapur Municipal Council. The number
and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval
of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Navapur Municipal
Council under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar
Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number
of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors :- 23
2. Two councillors wards :- 10
3. Three councillors wards :- 01
4. Total wards :- 11

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Sections 9, 10 and 341-B of The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its
authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council
area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

eastwards- along National Bakery, along Shri. Raj Ujjwal
Hospital upto S. No. 56 B up to the railway line.
East - From S. No. 56 B railwayline ,to the south along
Navapur city boundary upto S. No. 156, From S. No. 156
across MIDC Road to the south- up to S. No. 159.
South- From S. No. 159, to the west- including the
Municipal Council boundries, adjacent to the house of
Mr. Fatya Theganya Gavit in Kalamba (Pimpalner Road)
opposite Panchmukhi Hanuman and from behind the
house of Mr. Thakrya Rupji Gavit, along the southern wall
of the house, in the west including the Municipal Council

West- From Kalamba village (S.No No.-192), to the north
up to the drain in S. No. 255 (Gadad Road).
In case of any discrepancy in description of ward in the Marathi and English Notification, the
description as per boundaries shown in the map shall be treated as valid.
A map of the Navapur Municipal Council showing the limits of the wards is available during
office hours for inspection in the office of the Navapur Municipal Council.
